> Measurement caveat: the N100 suspended during the second session (session-1); telemetry contains a 32,279-second gap. All raw rows are retained. Clean replication is required before acceptance, especially for package energy.

# Direct GZRAM policy and block comparisons

Physical N100 warm-cache userspace screening. Nine paired rounds per session; median of three session changes. Session ranges are descriptive, not confidence intervals. Negative changes mean less time/space/energy. Comparisons across block sizes can change encoding choices and ratios. No policy or kernel gain accepted.
